Second:

```json
{"args": [{"args": [{"args": [{"const": "form_room"}, {"input": "f1116sb.prior_year"}], "test": "eq"}, {"args": [{"const": "other"}, {"input": "f1116sb.prior_year"}], "test": "eq"}], "test": "any"}, {"args": [{"const": 0}, {"line": "6 (xiii) and (xiv)"}], "test": "lt"}], "test": "all"}
```

Second transcription's note: A carryback requires 2024 to be an excess limitation year (Form 1116 line 24 less than line 23). No carryback goes to a year with no foreign income (Pub. 514, Example 2) or to an election year (i1116 Restrictions). Taxes deducted in 2024 ('other') still reduce the carryback by the amount that would have been used. The tool does not compute the carryback amount or the amended 2024 return, so line 7 refuses when 2024 is 'form_room' or 'other'.

Ruling: open

lines:
  - id: f1116sb.l1_i
    printed: "1 (i)"
    label: "Foreign tax carryover from the prior tax year (enter amounts from the appropriate columns of line 8 of the prior year Schedule B) — 10th Preceding Tax Year"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f1116sbyr, field: f1116sbyr.amount, where: {op: test, eq: [f1116sbyr.year, {const: "p10"}]}}
    note: "Open: whether the ftc_carry_year values count back from 2025 or name the 2024 Schedule B column. The instructions shift columns by one: line 1 column (i) takes line 8 column (ii) of the prior year. Chosen: p10 is the 10th tax year preceding 2025 (2015), which is column (i) of the 2025 schedule. The other columns follow the same pattern: p9 to (ii), and on through p1 to (xii)."
  - id: f1116sb.l1_ii
    printed: "1 (ii)"
    label: "Foreign tax carryover from the prior tax year — 9th Preceding Tax Year"

    printed: "1 (ix)"

    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f1116sbyr, field: f1116sbyr.amount, where: {op: test, eq: [f1116sbyr.year, {const: "p4"}]}}
  - id: f1116sb.l1_x
    printed: "1 (x)"
    label: "Foreign tax carryover from the prior tax year — 3rd Preceding Tax Year"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f1116sbyr, field: f1116sbyr.amount, where: {op: test, eq: [f1116sbyr.year, {const: "p3"}]}}
  - id: f1116sb.l1_xi
    printed: "1 (xi)"
    label: "Foreign tax carryover from the prior tax year — 2nd Preceding Tax Year"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f1116sbyr, field: f1116sbyr.amount, where: {op: test, eq: [f1116sbyr.year, {const: "p2"}]}}
  - id: f1116sb.l1_xii
    printed: "1 (xii)"
    label: "Foreign tax carryover from the prior tax year — 1st Preceding Tax Year"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f1116sbyr, field: f1116sbyr.amount, where: {op: test, eq: [f1116sbyr.year, {const: "p1"}]}}
  - id: f1116sb.h_has_carry
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Is there a foreign tax carryover from the prior tax year (line 1, column (xiv), more than zero)?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [f1116sb.l1_xiv, {const: 0}]}
  - id: f1116sb.changed_if_carry
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Adjustments to line 1 (line 2a carryback adjustment, line 2b section 905(c) redeterminations, lines 2c-2g domestic audit and other adjustments) are needed"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [f1116sb.h_has_carry, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [f1116sb.changed, {const: true}]}]}
    note: "Line 2 adjustments come from events between the filing of the prior-year return and this one. The tool does not compute them. Chosen: when the filer reports such a change and a carryover exists, line 3 refuses."
  - id: f1116sb.split_if_carry
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "An allocation between spouses (Pub. 514, Allocations Between Spouses) is needed for the carryover"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [f1116sb.h_has_carry, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [f1116sb.split, {const: true}]}]}
    note: "Pub. 514 requires the unused foreign tax of a joint-return year to be allocated between spouses who file separately. The tool does not compute this allocation, so line 3 refuses in that case."
  - id: f1116sb.l3_i
    printed: "3 (i)"
    label: "Adjusted foreign tax carryover from prior tax year (combine lines 1 and 2) — 10th Preceding Tax Year"

    refuses: [f1116sb.changed_if_carry, f1116sb.split_if_carry]
    note: "Line 2 is not computed: line 3 equals line 1 when no adjustment applies. The adjustment cases are refused."

    expr: {op: copy, from: f1116sb.l1_v}
    refuses: [f1116sb.changed_if_carry, f1116sb.split_if_carry]
  - id: f1116sb.l3_vi
    printed: "3 (vi)"
    label: "Adjusted foreign tax carryover from prior tax year (combine lines 1 and 2) — 5th Preceding Tax Year"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f1116sb.l1_vi}
    refuses: [f1116sb.changed_if_carry, f1116sb.split_if_carry]
  - id: f1116sb.l3_ix
    printed: "3 (ix)"
    label: "Adjusted foreign tax carryover from prior tax year (combine lines 1 and 2) — 4th Preceding Tax Year"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f1116sb.l1_ix}
    refuses: [f1116sb.changed_if_carry, f1116sb.split_if_carry]
  - id: f1116sb.l3_x
    printed: "3 (x)"
    label: "Adjusted foreign tax carryover from prior tax year (combine lines 1 and 2) — 3rd Preceding Tax Year"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f1116sb.l1_x}
    refuses: [f1116sb.changed_if_carry, f1116sb.split_if_carry]
  - id: f1116sb.l3_xi
    printed: "3 (xi)"
    label: "Adjusted foreign tax carryover from prior tax year (combine lines 1 and 2) — 2nd Preceding Tax Year"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f1116sb.l1_xi}
    refuses: [f1116sb.changed_if_carry, f1116sb.split_if_carry]
  - id: f1116sb.l3_xii
    printed: "3 (xii)"
    label: "Adjusted foreign tax carryover from prior tax year (combine lines 1 and 2) — 1st Preceding Tax Year"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f1116sb.l1_xii}
    refuses: [f1116sb.changed_if_carry, f1116sb.split_if_carry]
  - id: f1116sb.h_zero
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Zero"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f1116.zero}
  - id: f1116sb.h_current_taxes
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Current year foreign taxes available for credit (the sum of Form 1116, Part III, lines 9, 12, and 13)"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: f1116.line14, take: f1116.line10}
    note: "Open: the sign convention for 'the sum of lines 9, 12, and 13', since line 12 is a reduction. Chosen: combine lines 9, 12 and 13 as Form 1116 line 14 combines them. This is line 14 less line 10 (line 14 combines line 11 = 9 + 10 with lines 12 and 13)."
  - id: f1116sb.h_excess_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Current year excess limitation: the amount by which Form 1116, Part III, line 23 exceeds current year foreign taxes available for credit"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f1116.line23, take: f1116sb.h_current_taxes}
  - id: f1116sb.h_has_excess_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Do you have a current year excess limitation?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [f1116sb.h_excess_limit, {const: 0}]}
  - id: f1116sb.h_used_i
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Carryover used, column (i): line 3, column (i), but not more than the excess limitation"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [f1116sb.l3_i, f1116sb.h_excess_limit]}
  - id: f1116sb.h_rem_ii
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Excess limitation less the line 4 amounts in all previous columns (before column (ii))"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f1116sb.h_excess_limit, take: f1116sb.h_used_i}
  - id: f1116sb.h_used_ii
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Carryover used, column (ii)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [f1116sb.l3_ii, f1116sb.h_rem_ii]}
  - id: f1116sb.h_rem_iii
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Remaining excess limitation before column (iii)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f1116sb.h_rem_ii, take: f1116sb.h_used_ii}
  - id: f1116sb.h_used_iii
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Carryover used, column (iii)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [f1116sb.l3_iii, f1116sb.h_rem_iii]}
  - id: f1116sb.h_rem_iv
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Remaining excess limitation before column (iv)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f1116sb.h_rem_iii, take: f1116sb.h_used_iii}
  - id: f1116sb.h_used_iv
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Carryover used, column (iv)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [f1116sb.l3_iv, f1116sb.h_rem_iv]}
  - id: f1116sb.h_rem_v
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Remaining excess limitation before column (v)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f1116sb.h_rem_iv, take: f1116sb.h_used_iv}
  - id: f1116sb.h_used_v
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Carryover used, column (v)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [f1116sb.l3_v, f1116sb.h_rem_v]}
  - id: f1116sb.h_rem_vi
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Remaining excess limitation before column (vi)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f1116sb.h_rem_v, take: f1116sb.h_used_v}
  - id: f1116sb.h_used_vi
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Carryover used, column (vi)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [f1116sb.l3_vi, f1116sb.h_rem_vi]}
  - id: f1116sb.h_rem_ix
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Remaining excess limitation before column (ix)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f1116sb.h_rem_vi, take: f1116sb.h_used_vi}
  - id: f1116sb.h_used_ix
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Carryover used, column (ix)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [f1116sb.l3_ix, f1116sb.h_rem_ix]}
  - id: f1116sb.h_rem_x
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Remaining excess limitation before column (x)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f1116sb.h_rem_ix, take: f1116sb.h_used_ix}
  - id: f1116sb.h_used_x
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Carryover used, column (x)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [f1116sb.l3_x, f1116sb.h_rem_x]}
  - id: f1116sb.h_rem_xi
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Remaining excess limitation before column (xi)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f1116sb.h_rem_x, take: f1116sb.h_used_x}
  - id: f1116sb.h_used_xi
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Carryover used, column (xi)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [f1116sb.l3_xi, f1116sb.h_rem_xi]}
  - id: f1116sb.h_rem_xii
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Remaining excess limitation before column (xii)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f1116sb.h_rem_xi, take: f1116sb.h_used_xi}
  - id: f1116sb.h_used_xii
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Carryover used, column (xii)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [f1116sb.l3_xii, f1116sb.h_rem_xii]}
  - id: f1116sb.l4_i
    printed: "4 (i)"
    label: "Foreign tax carryover used in current tax year (enter as a negative number) — 10th Preceding Tax Year"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: f1116sb.h_zero, take: f1116sb.h_used_i}
    shown_when: f1116sb.h_has_excess_limit
  - id: f1116sb.l4_ii
    printed: "4 (ii)"
    label: "Foreign tax carryover used in current tax year (enter as a negative number) — 9th Preceding Tax Year"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: f1116sb.h_zero, take: f1116sb.h_used_ii}
    shown_when: f1116sb.h_has_excess_limit
  - id: f1116sb.l4_iii
    printed: "4 (iii)"
    label: "Foreign tax carryover used in current tax year (enter as a negative number) — 8th Preceding Tax Year"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: f1116sb.h_zero, take: f1116sb.h_used_iii}
    shown_when: f1116sb.h_has_excess_limit
  - id: f1116sb.l4_iv
    printed: "4 (iv)"
    label: "Foreign tax carryover used in current tax year (enter as a negative number) — 7th Preceding Tax Year"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: f1116sb.h_zero, take: f1116sb.h_used_iv}
    shown_when: f1116sb.h_has_excess_limit
  - id: f1116sb.l4_v
    printed: "4 (v)"
    label: "Foreign tax carryover used in current tax year (enter as a negative number) — 6th Preceding Tax Year"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: f1116sb.h_zero, take: f1116sb.h_used_v}
    shown_when: f1116sb.h_has_excess_limit
  - id: f1116sb.l4_vi
    printed: "4 (vi)"
    label: "Foreign tax carryover used in current tax year (enter as a negative number) — 5th Preceding Tax Year"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: f1116sb.h_zero, take: f1116sb.h_used_vi}
    shown_when: f1116sb.h_has_excess_limit
  - id: f1116sb.l4_ix
    printed: "4 (ix)"
    label: "Foreign tax carryover used in current tax year (enter as a negative number) — 4th Preceding Tax Year"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: f1116sb.h_zero, take: f1116sb.h_used_ix}
    shown_when: f1116sb.h_has_excess_limit
  - id: f1116sb.l4_x
    printed: "4 (x)"
    label: "Foreign tax carryover used in current tax year (enter as a negative number) — 3rd Preceding Tax Year"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: f1116sb.h_zero, take: f1116sb.h_used_x}
    shown_when: f1116sb.h_has_excess_limit
  - id: f1116sb.l4_xi
    printed: "4 (xi)"
    label: "Foreign tax carryover used in current tax year (enter as a negative number) — 2nd Preceding Tax Year"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: f1116sb.h_zero, take: f1116sb.h_used_xi}
    shown_when: f1116sb.h_has_excess_limit
  - id: f1116sb.l4_xii
    printed: "4 (xii)"
    label: "Foreign tax carryover used in current tax year (enter as a negative number) — 1st Preceding Tax Year"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: f1116sb.h_zero, take: f1116sb.h_used_xii}
    shown_when: f1116sb.h_has_excess_limit
  - id: f1116sb.l5_i
    printed: "5 (i), (vii), (viii) and (xiv)"
    label: "Foreign tax carryover expired unused in current tax year (enter as a negative number): for the 10th preceding tax year, combine lines 3 and 4"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: f1116sb.h_used_i, take: f1116sb.l3_i}
    note: "Line 5 is completed only for column (i) and for the subtotal and totals columns, so those columns all equal column (i). It is entered as a negative number: -(line 3 + line 4) = carryover used less line 3."
  - id: f1116sb.l6_xiii
    printed: "6 (xiii) and (xiv)"
    label: "Foreign tax carryover generated in current tax year (the amount by which the sum of Form 1116, Part III, lines 9, 12, and 13, exceeds the amount on Form 1116, Part III, line 23)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f1116sb.h_current_taxes, take: f1116.line23}
    shown_when: f1116sb.h_has_excess_tax
  - id: f1116sb.h_has_excess_tax
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Do you have current year excess foreign taxes?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [f1116sb.l6_xiii, {const: 0}]}
  - id: f1116sb.carryback
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Can any of line 6 be carried back to the first preceding tax year?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [f1116sb.h_has_excess_tax, {const: true}]}, {op: test, any: [{op: test, eq: [f1116sb.prior_year, {const: "form_room"}]}, {op: test, eq: [f1116sb.prior_year, {const: "other"}]}]}]}
    note: "A carryback requires 2024 to be an excess limitation year (Form 1116 line 24 less than line 23). No carryback goes to a year with no foreign income (Pub. 514, Example 2) or to an election year (i1116 Restrictions). Taxes deducted in 2024 ('other') still reduce the carryback by the amount that would have been used. The tool does not compute the carryback amount or the amended 2024 return, so line 7 refuses when 2024 is 'form_room' or 'other'."
  - id: f1116sb.l7_xiii
    printed: "7 (xiii) and (xiv)"
    label: "Actual or estimated amount of line 6 to be carried back to prior tax year (enter as a negative number)"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f1116sb.h_zero}
    refuses: [f1116sb.carryback]
    shown_when: f1116sb.h_has_excess_tax
  - id: f1116sb.l8_ii
    printed: "8 (ii)"
    label: "Foreign tax carryover to the following tax year. Combine lines 3 through 7. — 9th Preceding Tax Year"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f1116sb.l3_ii, take: f1116sb.h_used_ii}
  - id: f1116sb.l8_iii
    printed: "8 (iii)"
    label: "Foreign tax carryover to the following tax year. Combine lines 3 through 7. — 8th Preceding Tax Year"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f1116sb.l3_iii, take: f1116sb.h_used_iii}
  - id: f1116sb.l8_iv
    printed: "8 (iv)"
    label: "Foreign tax carryover to the following tax year. Combine lines 3 through 7. — 7th Preceding Tax Year"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f1116sb.l3_iv, take: f1116sb.h_used_iv}
  - id: f1116sb.l8_v
    printed: "8 (v)"
    label: "Foreign tax carryover to the following tax year. Combine lines 3 through 7. — 6th Preceding Tax Year"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f1116sb.l3_v, take: f1116sb.h_used_v}
  - id: f1116sb.l8_vi
    printed: "8 (vi)"
    label: "Foreign tax carryover to the following tax year. Combine lines 3 through 7. — 5th Preceding Tax Year"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f1116sb.l3_vi, take: f1116sb.h_used_vi}
  - id: f1116sb.l8_ix
    printed: "8 (ix)"
    label: "Foreign tax carryover to the following tax year. Combine lines 3 through 7. — 4th Preceding Tax Year"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f1116sb.l3_ix, take: f1116sb.h_used_ix}
  - id: f1116sb.l8_x
    printed: "8 (x)"
    label: "Foreign tax carryover to the following tax year. Combine lines 3 through 7. — 3rd Preceding Tax Year"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f1116sb.l3_x, take: f1116sb.h_used_x}
  - id: f1116sb.l8_xi
    printed: "8 (xi)"
    label: "Foreign tax carryover to the following tax year. Combine lines 3 through 7. — 2nd Preceding Tax Year"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f1116sb.l3_xi, take: f1116sb.h_used_xi}
  - id: f1116sb.l8_xii
    printed: "8 (xii)"
    label: "Foreign tax carryover to the following tax year. Combine lines 3 through 7. — 1st Preceding Tax Year"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f1116sb.l3_xii, take: f1116sb.h_used_xii}
  - id: f1116sb.l8_xiii
    printed: "8 (xiii)"
    label: "Foreign tax carryover to the following tax year. Combine lines 3 through 7. — Current Tax Year"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1116sb.l6_xiii, f1116sb.l7_xiii]}
  - id: f1116sb.l1_vii
    printed: "1 (vii) and (viii)"
    label: "Foreign tax carryover from the prior tax year — Subtotal (add columns (i) through (vi))"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1116sb.l1_i, f1116sb.l1_ii, f1116sb.l1_iii, f1116sb.l1_iv, f1116sb.l1_v, f1116sb.l1_vi]}
  - id: f1116sb.l1_xiv
    printed: "1 (xiv)"
    label: "Foreign tax carryover from the prior tax year — Totals (add columns (viii) through (xiii))"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1116sb.l1_vii, f1116sb.l1_ix, f1116sb.l1_x, f1116sb.l1_xi, f1116sb.l1_xii]}
  - id: f1116sb.l3_vii
    printed: "3 (vii) and (viii)"
    label: "Adjusted foreign tax carryover from prior tax year — Subtotal (add columns (i) through (vi))"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1116sb.l3_i, f1116sb.l3_ii, f1116sb.l3_iii, f1116sb.l3_iv, f1116sb.l3_v, f1116sb.l3_vi]}
  - id: f1116sb.l3_xiv
    printed: "3 (xiv)"
    label: "Adjusted foreign tax carryover from prior tax year — Totals (add columns (viii) through (xiii)). Include the column (xiv) total on the current year Form 1116, Part III, line 10."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1116sb.l3_vii, f1116sb.l3_ix, f1116sb.l3_x, f1116sb.l3_xi, f1116sb.l3_xii]}
  - id: f1116sb.l4_vii
    printed: "4 (vii) and (viii)"
    label: "Foreign tax carryover used in current tax year — Subtotal (add columns (i) through (vi))"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1116sb.l4_i, f1116sb.l4_ii, f1116sb.l4_iii, f1116sb.l4_iv, f1116sb.l4_v, f1116sb.l4_vi]}
    shown_when: f1116sb.h_has_excess_limit
  - id: f1116sb.l4_xiv
    printed: "4 (xiv)"
    label: "Foreign tax carryover used in current tax year — Totals (add columns (viii) through (xiii)); can't exceed the current year excess limitation"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1116sb.l4_vii, f1116sb.l4_ix, f1116sb.l4_x, f1116sb.l4_xi, f1116sb.l4_xii]}
    shown_when: f1116sb.h_has_excess_limit
  - id: f1116sb.l8_vii
    printed: "8 (vii) and (viii)"
    label: "Foreign tax carryover to the following tax year — Subtotal (add columns (i) through (vi)); column (i) is -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1116sb.l8_ii, f1116sb.l8_iii, f1116sb.l8_iv, f1116sb.l8_v, f1116sb.l8_vi]}
    note: "Line 8, column (i) is preprinted -0-, because combining lines 3, 4 and 5 in that column always gives zero."
  - id: f1116sb.l8_xiv
    printed: "8 (xiv)"
    label: "Foreign tax carryover to the following tax year — Totals (add columns (viii) through (xiii))"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1116sb.l8_vii, f1116sb.l8_ix, f1116sb.l8_x, f1116sb.l8_xi, f1116sb.l8_xii, f1116sb.l8_xiii]}
